## Combo 2099-4694-5805-7306
Cards: The Ooze + Sarcatog + March of the Machines + Gev, Scaled Scorch
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature tokens; Infinite artifact tokens; Infinitely large creature until end of turn; Infinite Mutagen tokens; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers
Start: The Ooze (battlefield); Sarcatog (battlefield); March of the Machines (battlefield); Gev, Scaled Scorch (battlefield)
Prerequisites: At least one opponent has lost life this turn.
  You control at least one additional creature with a +1/+1 counter on it.
Steps: Activate Sarcatog by sacrificing a creature with a +1/+1 counter on it.
  When that creature dies, The Ooze triggers, creating a Mutagen artifact token for each +1/+1 counter on the sacrificed creature. These artifacts are also 0/0 creatures due to March of the Machines.
  Each Mutagen artifact creature token enters with at least one +1/+1 counter on it due to Gev.
  Resolve the Sarcatog ability, giving it +1/+1 until end of turn.
  Repeat from step 1.

Start: Dross Scorpion (battlefield); Detective's Satchel (battlefield); Sarcatog (battlefield)

  Repeat.

  Each Mutagen artifact creature token enters with at least one +1/+1 counter on it due to Gev.
  Resolve the Megatog ability, giving it +3/+3 and trample until end of turn.
  Repeat from step 1.

  Consecrated Sphinx triggers, causing the opponent to draw two cards due to Notion Thief.
  Consecrated Sphinx triggers twice.
  Resolve the first trigger, causing the opponent to draw two cards due to Notion Thief.
  Repeat from step 4 until the opponent loses the game due to drawing from an empty library.

Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {5}{B}{B}
Produces: Infinite self-mill; Infinite red mana; Put all artifact creature cards with mana value 2 or less from your library onto the battlefield; Put all artifact creature cards with mana value 2 or less from your graveyard onto the battlefield; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers
Start: Technomancer (hand); Phyrexian Metamorph (graveyard); Thermopod (battlefield)
Steps: Cast Technomancer by paying {5}{B}{B}.
  When Technomancer enters, it triggers, causing you to mill three cards and return Phyrexian Metamorph from your graveyard to the battlefield as a copy of Technomancer.
  When Phyrexian Metamorph enters, it triggers. Holding priority, activate Thermopod by sacrificing Phyrexian Metamorph.
  Resolve the Phyrexian Metamorph trigger, causing you to mill three cards and return Phyrexian Metamorph from your graveyard to the battlefield as a copy of Technomancer.
  Repeat from step 3.

## Combo 3742-3991-4846
Cards: Worldgorger Dragon + Phantom Steed + Faceless Butcher
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 1 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the mana cost of the creature in your hand
Produces: Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite blinking of permanents
Start: Worldgorger Dragon (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Phantom Steed (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Faceless Butcher (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield)
Prerequisites: Faceless Butcher must be exiled by Phantom Steed, and Worldgorger Dragon must begin in hand. (This starting order is not fixed, and their initial positions may be interchanged as necessary.)
Steps: Cast Worldgorger Dragon for its mana cost.
  When Worldgorger Dragon enters, it exiles Phantom Steed until Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Worldgorger Dragon's ETB.
  When Phantom Steed leaves the battlefield, Faceless Butcher is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Faceless Butcher enters, it exiles Worldgorger Dragon until Faceless Butcher leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Faceless Butcher's ETB.
  When Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield, Phantom Steed is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Phantom Steed enters, it exiles Faceless Butcher until Phantom Steed leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Phantom Steed's ETB.
  When Faceless Butcher leaves the battlefield, Worldgorger Dragon is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: Due to differences in card templating between the various banisher effects (linked triggered abilities vs "until" clause), steps 3, 5, and 7 may return the creature to the battlefield during the resolution of the preceding step rather than creating a separate event. This should not affect the overall execution of the combo, only how many objects pass through the stack (and thus rounds of priority available per loop).

## Combo 3749-4067-4653
Cards: Lagomos, Hand of Hatred + Mind Over Matter + Dread
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite self-discard triggers
Start: Lagomos, Hand of Hatred (battlefield); Mind Over Matter (battlefield); Dread (library)

  Lagomos does not have summoning sickness.
Steps: Activate Lagomos by tapping it, searching your library for Dread and putting it into your hand.

  Repeat.

## Combo 3750-3898
Cards: Cyclonus, the Saboteur // Cyclonus, Cybertronian Fighter + Aggravated Assault
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 110 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite card draw; Near-infinite combat damage; Near-infinite combat phases; Near-infinite mana permanents you control can produce; Near-infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature; Near-infinite untap of permanents you control; Infinite draw triggers; Near-infinite self-discard triggers
Start: Cyclonus, the Saboteur // Cyclonus, Cybertronian Fighter (battlefield); Aggravated Assault (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Cyclonus has power 5 or greater and does not have summoning sickness.
  Permanents you control can tap to produce at least {6}{R}{R}{R}{R}.
  An opponent cannot block Cyclonus.
Steps: Deal combat damage to an opponent using Cyclonus, the Saboteur.
  Cyclonus triggers, causing it to connive and convert it to Cyclonus, Cybertronian Fighter.
  Move to your postcombat main phase.
  Activate any number of permanents you control by tapping them, adding at least {3}{R}{R}.
  Activate Aggravated Assault by paying {3}{R}{R}, causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat and main phase after this one.
  Move to your next combat phase, and deal combat damage to an opponent using Cyclonus, Cybertronian Fighter.
  Cyclonus triggers, causing you to convert it to Cyclonus, the Saboteur and to get an additional beginning phase after this one.
  Move to your beginning phase, causing you to untap all permanents you control.
  Move to your postcombat main phase.
  Activate any number of permanents you control by tapping them, adding at least {3}{R}{R}.
  Activate Aggravated Assault by paying {3}{R}{R}, causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat and main phase after this one.
  Move to your next combat phase.
  Repeat.

## Combo 3769-3862-8412
Cards: Orcish Bowmasters + Blim, Comedic Genius + Ruric Thar, Biomagus
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {1}{B}
Produces: Infinite card draw for target opponent; Target opponent loses the game; Infinite draw triggers for target opponent
Start: Orcish Bowmasters (hand); Blim, Comedic Genius (battlefield); Ruric Thar, Biomagus (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Blim does not have summoning sickness and cannot be blocked by an opponent.

  Ruric Thar's last ability triggers, causing its controller to draw a card.
  Orcish Bowmasters triggers, targeting Ruric Thar.
  Repeat from step 5 until the opponent loses the game due to drawing from an empty library.
Notes: Once the opponent loses the game, all Orcish Bowmasters abilities will fizzle as they no longer have legal targets.

  Cast Orcish Bowmasters by paying {1}{B}.
  When Orcish Bowmasters enters, it triggers, targeting Ruric Thar.
  Ruric Thar's last ability triggers, causing its controller to draw a card.
  Orcish Bowmasters triggers, targeting Ruric Thar.
  Repeat from step 4 until the opponent loses the game due to drawing from an empty library.
Notes: Once the opponent loses the game, all Orcish Bowmasters abilities will fizzle as they no longer have legal targets.

## Combo 3991-4142-4846
Cards: Worldgorger Dragon + Faceless Butcher + Hostage Taker
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 1 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the mana cost of the creature in your hand
Produces: Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite blinking of permanents
Start: Worldgorger Dragon (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Faceless Butcher (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Hostage Taker (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield)
Prerequisites: Hostage Taker must be exiled by Faceless Butcher, and Worldgorger Dragon must begin in hand. (This starting order is not fixed, and their initial positions may be interchanged as necessary.)
Steps: Cast Worldgorger Dragon for its mana cost.
  When Worldgorger Dragon enters, it exiles Faceless Butcher until Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Worldgorger Dragon's ETB.
  When Faceless Butcher leaves the battlefield, Hostage Taker is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Hostage Taker enters, it exiles Worldgorger Dragon until Hostage Taker leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Hostage Taker's ETB.
  When Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield, Faceless Butcher is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Faceless Butcher enters, it exiles Hostage Taker until Faceless Butcher leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Faceless Butcher's ETB.
  When Hostage Taker leaves the battlefield, Worldgorger Dragon is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: Due to differences in card templating between the various banisher effects (linked triggered abilities vs "until" clause), steps 3, 5, and 7 may return the creature to the battlefield during the resolution of the preceding step rather than creating a separate event. This should not affect the overall execution of the combo, only how many objects pass through the stack (and thus rounds of priority available per loop).

Produces: Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers
Start: Wick, the Whorled Mind (hand); Leyline of Transformation (battlefield: with "Rat" chosen); Phantom Train (battlefield)
Steps: Cast Wick by paying {3}{B}.
  When Wick enters the battlefield, it triggers.
  Holding priority, if you control a Snail, activate Phantom Train by sacrificing the Snail, putting a +1/+1 counter on Phantom Train.
  If needed, repeat step 3 until you do not control a Snail.
  Resolve the Wick trigger, creating a 1/1 Snail creature token.
  When the Snail enters the battlefield as a Rat due to Leyline of Transformation, Wick triggers.
  Repeat from step 3.

## Combo 6249-6495-7920--5
Cards: Olivia, Mobilized for War + Evereth, Viceroy of Plunder + Tresserhorn's Lord, Returned
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Near-infinite damage; Infinite looting; Near-infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature; Infinite draw triggers; Infinite self-discard triggers; Near-infinite creature ETB; Near-infinite creature LTB; Near-infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Near-infinite death triggers
Requires: Persist Creature (battlefield: without a -1/-1 counter on it)
Start: Olivia, Mobilized for War (battlefield); Evereth, Viceroy of Plunder (battlefield); Tresserhorn's Lord, Returned (battlefield)
Steps: Activate Evereth by sacrificing the persist creature.
  When the persist creature dies, it and Tresserhorn's Lord trigger.
  Resolve the Tresserhorn's Lord trigger, causing you to draw a card.
  Resolve the persist creature's trigger, returning it from your graveyard to the battlefield with a -1/-1 counter on it.
  When the persist creature enters the battlefield, Olivia triggers, putting a +1/+1 counter on the persist creature and causing you to discard a card.
  Resolve the Evereth ability.
  Repeat.
Notes: If you have a way to kill Evereth and {1}{B/R} available, you can do so after putting a significant amount of +1/+1 counters on it to deal damage to each opponent using its second ability.

## Combo 6428-6574
Cards: Magmakin Artillerist + Kefka, Court Mage // Kefka, Ruler of Ruin
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 6700 | Commander: legal
Produces: Near-infinite damage; Infinite rummaging; Infinite self-discard triggers; Infinite draw triggers
Start: Magmakin Artillerist (battlefield); Kefka, Court Mage // Kefka, Ruler of Ruin (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have a number of cards in your hand that is greater than your maximum hand size.
Steps: Proceed to your cleanup step, causing you to discard down to your maximum hand size.
  Magmakin Artillerist triggers for each card discarded this way.
  Resolve the first Magmakin Artillerist trigger, dealing damage to each opponent.
  Kefka triggers, causing you to draw a card for each opponent that lost life.
  Repeat from step 3 as needed for all remaining Magmakin Artillerist triggers.
  Once all players have passed priority and the stack is empty, you will still be in your cleanup step, and will discard down to your maximum hand size.
  Repeat from step 2.

## Combo 8195-8423--222
Cards: Venser, Fervent Forger + The Trickster-God's Heist
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: 2-3+ (Oddball) | Popularity: 4 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {2}{U}{B} and {0} plus the cost of copying Venser
Produces: Infinite copies of permanents opponents control; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers
Requires: Instant or Sorcery that Creates One or More Copies of an Opposing Creature (hand)
Start: Venser, Fervent Forger (battlefield); The Trickster-God's Heist (hand)
Prerequisites: An opponent controls a creature.
Steps: Cast The Trickster God's Heist by paying {2}{U}{B}, putting a lore counter on it as it enters and causing its first chapter ability to trigger.
  Resolve the chapter ability, exchanging control of a creature an opponent controls and Venser.
  Cast your copy spell from the templated list by paying its mana cost, choosing the necessary targets to create at least one copy of Venser.
  Each Venser that entered triggers. Put those triggers on the stack, choosing the second mode and targeting the Venser your opponent controls for at least one copy.
  Resolve a Venser trigger, creating two copies of a permanent an opponent controls (this must be Venser if you only put one trigger on the stack in Step 4).
  Repeat Step 5 until you have created two copies of Venser.
  Repeat from Step 4. Depending on the permanents targeted in Step 5, the features produced will change.
